\"\"\"\n        if not self.use_champion or self.predictor is None:\n            # Legacy fallback\n            return self._legacy_predict(player_data)\n        \n        features = self.extract_features(player_data)\n        features = features.reshape(1, -1)\n        \n        prediction, confidence = self.predictor.predict_with_confidence(features)\n        return float(prediction[0]), float(confidence[0])\n    \n    def predict_batch(self, players_df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n        \"\"\"\n        Predict points for multiple players.\n        \n        Args:\n            players_df: DataFrame with player data\n            \n        Returns:\n            DataFrame with 'predicted_points' and 'confidence' columns added\n        \"\"\"\n        if not self.use_champion or self.predictor is None:\n            logger.warning(\"Champion model not available, using legacy predictions\")\n            return self._legacy_predict_batch(players_df)\n        \n        # Extract features for all players\n        features_list = []\n        for _, player in players_df.iterrows():\n            features = self.extract_features(player.to_dict())\n            features_list.append(features)\n        \n        X = np.array(features_list)\n        \n        # Generate predictions\n        predictions, confidence = self.predictor.predict_with_confidence(X)\n        \n        # Add to dataframe\n        result = players_df.copy()\n        result['predicted_points'] = predictions\n        result['confidence'] = confidence\n        result['model_version'] = 'EXP-030-champion'\n        \n        return result\n    \n    def predict_for_gameweek(\n        self,\n        gameweek: int,\n        fpl_data: Optional[Dict] = None\n    ) -> pd.DataFrame:\n        \"\"\"\n        Predict points for all players in a gameweek.\n        \n        Args:\n            gameweek: Target gameweek\n            fpl_data: FPL data dict (if None, fetches from API)\n            \n        Returns:\n            DataFrame with predictions for all active players\n        \"\"\"\n        logger.info(f\"Generating predictions for GW{gameweek}...\")\n        \n        # Fetch data if not provided\n        if fpl_data is None:\n            try:\n                from fpl_service import FPLService\n                fpl_service = FPLService()\n                fpl_data = fpl_service.get_latest_data()\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.error(f\"Failed to fetch FPL data: {e}\")\n                return pd.DataFrame()\n        \n        # Convert to DataFrame\n        players = []\n        for element in fpl_data['static']['elements']:\n            player = {\n                'id': element['id'],\n                'name': element['web_name'],\n                'team': element['team'],\n                'position': element['element_type'],\n                'price': element['now_cost'] / 10.0,\n                'form': float(element.get('form', 0) or 0),\n                'total_points': element['total_points'],\n                'points_per_game': float(element.get('points_per_game', 0) or 0),\n                'minutes': element['minutes'],\n                'goals_scored': element['goals_scored'],\n                'assists': element['assists'],\n                'clean_sheets': element['clean_sheets'],\n                'goals_conceded': element['goals_conceded'],\n                'bonus': element['bonus'],\n                'bps': element['bps'],\n                'influence': float(element.get('influence', 0) or 0),\n                'creativity': float(element.get('creativity', 0) or 0),\n                'threat': float(element.get('threat', 0) or 0),\n                'ict_index': float(element.get('ict_index', 0) or 0),\n                'selected_by_percent': float(element.get('selected_by_percent', 0) or 0),\n                'transfers_in': element['transfers_in'],\n                'transfers_out': element['transfers_out'],\n                'value_season': float(element.get('value_season', 0) or 0),\n                'value_form': float(element.get('value_form', 0) or 0),\n            }\n            players.append(player)\n        \n        df = pd.DataFrame(players)\n        \n        # Add fixture difficulty\n        df['fixture_difficulty'] = self._calculate_fixture_difficulty(df, fpl_data, gameweek)\n        df['opponent_strength'] = df['fixture_difficulty']\n        \n        # Calculate derived features\n        df['recent_form_3gw'] = df['form'] * 0.7  # Simplified\n        df['recent_form_5gw'] = df['form'] * 0.9  # Simplified\n        df['consistency_score'] = df.apply(\n            lambda x: min(x['minutes'] / 90, 1.0) if x['minutes'] > 0 else 0, \n            axis=1\n        )\n        \n        # Generate predictions\n        result = self.predict_batch(df)\n        \n        logger.info(f\"Generated predictions for {len(result)} players\")\n        logger.info(f\"  Mean prediction: {result['predicted_points'].mean():.2f}\")\n        logger.info(f\"  Top predicted: {result.nlargest(3, 'predicted_points')[['name', 'predicted_points']].to_dict('records')}\")\n        \n        return result\n    \n    def _calculate_fixture_difficulty(self, players_df, fpl_data, gameweek):\n        \"\"\"Calculate fixture difficulty for each player.\"\"\"\n        # Simplified - in reality would use fixture data\n        difficulties = []\n        for _, player in players_df.iterrows():\n            # Default to medium difficulty\n            difficulties.append(3.0)\n        return difficulties\n    \n    def _legacy_predict(self, player_data: Dict) -> Tuple[float, float]:\n        \"\"\"Legacy prediction method (fallback).\"\"\"\n        # Simple heuristic fallback\n        form = float(player_data.get('form', 0) or 0)\n        ppg = float(player_data.get('points_per_game', 0) or 0)\n        ict = float(player_data.get('ict_index', 0) or 0)\n        \n        prediction = form * 0.4 + ppg * 0.4 + ict * 0.02\n        confidence = 0.5  # Low confidence for legacy\n        \n        return max(0, prediction), confidence\n    \n    def _legacy_predict_batch(self, players_df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:\n        \"\"\"Legacy batch prediction (fallback).\"\"\"\n        result = players_df.copy()\n        predictions = []\n        confidences = []\n        \n        for _, player in players_df.iterrows():\n            pred, conf = self._legacy_predict(player.to_dict())\n            predictions.append(pred)\n            confidences.append(conf)\n        \n        result['predicted_points'] = predictions\n        result['confidence'] = confidences\n        result['model_version'] = 'legacy-fallback'\n        \n        return result\n    \n    def get_model_status(self) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Get current model status.\"\"\"\n        return {\n global champion predictor instance.\"\"\"\n    global _champion_predictor\n    if _champion_predictor is None:\n        _champion_predictor = ChampionPointPredictor()\n    return _champion_predictor\n\n\ndef predict_gameweek(gameweek: int, fpl_data: Optional[Dict] = None) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"\n    Convenience function to predict a gameweek.\n    \n    Args:\n {
  "path": "/home/akshit/fpl-lineup-optimizer/run_team_prediction.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nRun prediction for a specific FPL team.\n\nUsage:\n    python run_team_prediction.py <team_id> [--transfers N] [--balance X.X]\n\nExample:\n    python run_team_prediction.py 9777842 --transfers 5 --balance 1.9\n\"\"\"\n\nimport sys\nimport json\nimport requests\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\n# Add backend to path\nsys.path.insert(0, '/home/akshit/fpl-lineup-optimizer/backend')\n\ntry:\n    from production_predictor import ProductionPredictor\n    from champion_predictor_integration import ChampionPointPredictor\n    CHAMPION_AVAILABLE = True\nexcept ImportError as e:\n    print(f\"Warning: Could not import champion predictor: {e}\")\n    CHAMPION_AVAILABLE = False\n\n\nclass FPLTeamAnalyzer:\n    \"\"\"Analyze an FPL team and generate predictions.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, team_id):\n        self.team_id = team_id\n        self.session = requests.Session()\n        self.session.headers.update({\n            'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36'\n        })\n        \n        self.static_data = None\n        self.team_data = None\n        self.current_gw = None\n        \n    def fetch_static_data(self):\n        \"\"\"Fetch FPL static data.\"\"\"\n        print(\"📡 Fetching FPL static data...\")\n        response = self.session.get('https://fantasy.premierleague.com/api/bootstrap-static/')\n        response.raise_for_status()\n        self.static_data = response.json()\n        \n        # Find current gameweek\n        for gw in self.static_data['events']:\n            if gw['is_current']:\n                self.current_gw = gw['id']\n                break\n        if not self.current_gw:\n            self.current_gw = max(gw['id'] for gw in self.static_data['events'] if gw['finished'])\n            \n        print(f\"   Current GW: {self.current_gw}\")\n        return self.static_data\n    \n    def fetch_team_data(self):\n        \"\"\"Fetch team data.\"\"\"\n        print(f\"📡 Fetching team {self.team_id} data...\")\n        \n        # Try without auth first (public endpoint for current GW)\n        url = f'https://fantasy.premierleague.com/api/entry/{self.team_id}/event/{self.current_gw}/picks/'\n        \n        try:\n            response = self.session.get(url)\n            response.raise_for_status()\n            picks_data = response.json()\n            \n            # Get team info\n            team_info_url = f'https://fantasy.premierleague.com/api/entry/{self.team_id}/'\n            team_info = self.session.get(team_info_url).json()\n            \n            self.team_data = {\n                'picks': picks_data['picks'],\n                'entry_history': picks_data['entry_history'],\n                'team_info': team_info\n            }\n            \n            print(f\"   Team: {team_info.get('player_first_name', '')} {team_info.get('player_last_name', '')}\")\n            print(f\"   Team Name: {team_info.get('name', 'Unknown')}\")\n            print(f\"   Overall Rank: {team_info.get('summary_overall_rank', 'N/A'):,}\")\n            print(f\"   Total Points: {team_info.get('summary_overall_points', 'N/A')}\")\n            \n            return self.team_data\n            \n        except requests.exceptions.HTTPError as e:\n            if e.response.status_code == 404:\n                print(f\"   Error: Team {self.team_id} not found or no data for GW{self.current_gw}\")\n                print(f\"   Trying to fetch team history...\")\n                return self._fetch_team_history()\n            raise\n    \n    def _fetch_team_history(self):\n        \"\"\"Fetch team history as fallback.\"\"\"\n        url = f'https://fantasy.premierleague.com/api/entry/{self.team_id}/history/'\n        response = self.session.get(url)\n        response.raise_for_status()\n        history = response.json()\n        \n        # Get latest GW picks\n        if history.get('current'):\n            latest = history['current'][-1]\n            print(f\"   Latest GW with data: GW{latest['event']}\")\n            \n        # Get team info\n        team_info_url = f'https://fantasy.premierleague.com/api/entry/{self.team_id}/'\n        team_info = self.session.get(team_info_url).json()\n        \n        print(f\"   Team: {team_info.get('name', 'Unknown')}\")\n        \n        self.team_data = {\n            'history': history,\n            'team_info': team_info\n        }\n        \n        return self.team_data\n    \n    def get_squad_details(self):\n        \"\"\"Get detailed squad information.\"\"\"\n        if not self.team_data or not self.static_data:\n            raise ValueError(\"Fetch data first\")\n        \n        # Get player picks\n        if 'picks' in self.team_data:\n            picks = self.team_data['picks']\n        else:\n            # Use history fallback - need to simulate from transfers\n            print(\"   Note: Using historical data (transfers may have occurred)\")\n            return self._build_squad_from_scratch()\n        \n        # Build squad details\n        squad = []\n        elements = {p['id']: p for p in self.static_data['elements']}\n        teams = {t['id']: t for t in self.static_data['teams']}\n        \n        for pick in picks:\n            player_id = pick['element']\n            player = elements.get(player_id)\n            \n            if player:\n                squad.append({\n                    'id': player_id,\n                    'name': player['web_name'],\n                    'position': player['element_type'],  # 1=GK, 2=DEF, 3=MID, 4=FWD\n                    'team': teams.get(player['team'], {}).get('name', 'Unknown'),\n                    'price': player['now_cost'] / 10.0,\n                    'form': float(player.get('form', 0) or 0),\n                    'total_points': player['total_points'],\n                    'points_per_game': float(player.get('points_per_game', 0) or 0),\n                    'minutes': player['minutes'],\n                    'goals_scored': player['goals_scored'],\n                    'assists': player['assists'],\n                    'clean_sheets': player['clean_sheets'],\n                    'bonus': player['bonus'],\n                    'bps': player['bps'],\n                    'influence': float(player.get('influence', 0) or 0),\n                    'creativity': float(player.get('creativity', 0) or 0),\n                    'threat': float(player.get('threat', 0) or 0),\n                    'ict_index': float(player.get('ict_index', 0) or 0),\n                    'selected_by_percent': float(player.get('selected_by_percent', 0) or 0),\n                    'transfers_in': player['transfers_in'],\n                    'transfers_out': player['transfers_out'],\n                    'is_captain': pick.get('is_captain', False),\n                    'is_vice_captain': pick.get('is_vice_captain', False),\n                    'multiplier': pick.get('multiplier', 1),\n                })\n        \n        return squad\n    \n    def _build_squad_from_scratch(self):\n        \"\"\"Build a sample squad for testing.\"\"\"\n        print(\"   Building sample squad for analysis...\")\n        \n        # Get top players by form\n        elements = self.static_data['elements']\n        \n        # Simple position mapping\n        gks = [p for p in elements if p['element_type'] == 1]\n        defs = [p for p in elements if p['element_type'] == 2]\n        mids = [p for p in elements if p['element_type'] == 3]\n        fwds = [p for p in elements if p['element_type'] == 4]\n        \n        # Sort by form\n        gks = sorted(gks, key=lambda x: float(x.get('form', 0) or 0), reverse=True)\n        defs = sorted(defs, key=lambda x: float(x.get('form', 0) or 0), reverse=True)\n        mids = sorted(mids, key=lambda x: float(x.get('form', 0) or 0), reverse=True)\n        fwds = sorted(fwds, key=lambda x: float(x.get('form', 0) or 0), reverse=True)\n        \n        teams = {t['id']: t for t in self.static_data['teams']}\n        \n        squad = []\n        selected = []\n        \n        # Set captain to highest form\n        squad[4]['is_captain'] = True\n        squad[4]['multiplier'] = 2\n        \n        return squad\n    \n    def predict_with_champion(self, squad):\n        \"\"\"Predict points using champion model.\"\"\"\n        if not CHAMPION_AVAILABLE:\n            print(\"   Champion model not available, using form-based estimates\")\n            return [(p, p['form'], 0.5) for p in squad]\n        \n        print(\"🔮 Running champion model predictions...\")\n        \n        predictor = ChampionPointPredictor()\n        \n        predictions = []\n        for player in squad:\n            try:\n                pred, conf = predictor.predict_player(player)\n                predictions.append((player, pred, conf))\n            except Exception as e:\n                # player['total_points'],\n                    'minutes': player['minutes'],\n                    'bonus': player['bonus'],\n                    'bps': player['bps'],\n                    'is_captain': pick.get('is_captain', False),\n                    'is_vice_captain': pick.get('is_vice_captain', False),\n                    'multiplier': pick.get('multiplier', 1),\n                    'status': player.get('status', 'a'),\n                    'news': player.get('news', ''),\n                })\n        \n        return squad\n    \n    def suggest_lineup(self, squad):\n        print(\"\\n🎯 OPTIMAL LINEUP SUGGESTION\")\n        print(\"=\"*70)\n        \n        gks = [p for p in squad if p['position'] == 1]\n        defs = [p for p in squad if p['position'] == 2]\n        mids = [p for p in squad if p['position'] == 3]\n        fwds = [p for p in squad if p['position'] == 4]\n        \n        gks = sorted(gks, key=lambda x: x['predicted_points'], reverse=True)\n        defs = sorted(defs, key=lambda x: x['predicted_points'], reverse=True)\n        mids = sorted(mids, key=lambda x: x['predicted_points'], reverse=True)\n        fwds = sorted(fwds, key=lambda x: x['predicted_points'], reverse=True)\n        \n        # Try formations\n        formations = [(3,4,3), (3,5,2), (4,3,3), (4,4,2), (4,5,1), (5,3,2), (5,4,1)]\n        \n        best = None\n        best_points = 0\n        \n        for n_def, n_mid, n_fwd in formations:\n            if len(defs) < n_def or len(mids) < n_mid or len(fwds) < n_fwd:\n                continue\n            \n            lineup = gks[:1] + defs[:n_def] + mids[:n_mid] + fwds[:n_fwd]\n            \n            # Captain is highest predicted\n            captain = max(lineup, key=lambda x: x['predicted_points'])\n            total = sum(p['predicted_points'] for p in lineup) + captain['predicted_points']\n            \n            if total > best_points:\n                best_points = total\n                best = (n_def, n_mid, n_fwd, lineup, captain)\n        \n        if best:\n            n_def, n_mid, n_fwd, lineup, captain = best\n            print(f\"\\nBest Formation: {n_def}-{n_mid}-{n_fwd}\")\n            print(f\"Expected Points: {best_points:.1f}\\n\")\n            \n            pos_names = {1: 'GK', 2: 'DEF', 3: 'MID', 4: 'FWD'}\n            \n            print(f\"{'Pos':<5} {'Player':<25} {'Team':<6} {'£':<6} {'Form':<6} {'xPts':<6}\")\n            print(\"-\"*70)\n            \n            for p in sorted(lineup, key=lambda x: (x['position'], -x['predicted_points'])):\n                pos = pos_names[p['position']]\n                marker = \" (C)\" if p['is_captain'] else \" (V)\" if p['is_vice_captain'] else \"\"\n                print(f\"{pos:<5} {p['name']:<25} {p['team']:<6} {p['price']:<6.1f} {p['form']:<6.1f} {p['predicted_points']:<5.1f}{marker}\")\n            \n            # Bench\n            bench = [p for p in squad if p not in lineup]\n            if bench:\n                print(\"\\nBench:\")\n                for p in bench:\n                    pos = pos_names[p['position']]\n                    print(f\"{pos:<5} {p['name']:<25} {p['team']:<6} {p['price']:<6.1f} {p['form']:<6.1f} {p['predicted_points']:<5.1f}\")\n            \n            return best\n        return None\n    \n    def suggest_transfers(self, squad, transfers, balance):\n        print(\"\\n🔄 TRANSFER RECOMMENDATIONS\")\n        print(\"=\"*70)\n        print(f\"Available: {transfers} transfers | Bank: £{balance}m\\n\")\n        \n        if transfers == 0:\n            print(\"No transfers available. Consider saving for future GWs.\")\n            return\n        \n        # Sort by predicted points (lowest first = sell candidates)\n        sorted_squad = sorted(squad, key=lambda x: x['predicted_points'])\n        \n        print(\"Players to CONSIDER SELLING (low expected returns):\")\n        print(f\"{'Player':<25} {'Team':<6} {'Price':<7} {'Form':<6} {'xPts':<6} {'Status'}\")\n        print(\"-\"*75)\n        \n        for p in sorted_squad[:transfers]:\n            status = \"🔴 \" + p['news'][:30] if p['news'] else \"🟢 Available\"\n            print(f\"{p['name']:<25} {p['team']:<6} £{p['price']:<6.1f} {p['form']:<6.1f} {p['predicted_points']:<5.1f} {status}\")\n        \n        # Top targets from all players\n        all_players = self.static_data['elements']\n        available = [p for p in all_players if p.get('status') == 'a']  # Available\n        \n        # Filter by budget\n        max_price = sorted_squad[0]['price'] + balance if sorted_squad else 10.0\n        \n        targets = []\n        for p in available:\n            if p['now_cost'] / 10.0 > max_price + 0.1:\n                continue\n            \n            form = float(p.get('form', 0) or 0)\n            ppg = float(p.get('points_per_game', 0) or 0)\n            ict = float(p.get('ict_index', 0) or 0)\n            \n            pred = form * 0.5 + ppg * 0.3 + ict * 0.05\n            \n            if pred > sorted_squad[0]['predicted_points'] + 1:  # Significant upgrade\n                targets.append({\n                    'name': p['web_name'],\n                    'team': self.static_data['teams'][p['team']-1]['short_name'] if p['team'] <= len(self.static_data['teams']) else 'UNK',\n                    'position': p['element_type'],\n                    'price': p['now_cost'] / 10.0,\n                    'form': form,\n                    'predicted_points': pred\n                })\n        \n        targets = sorted(targets, key=lambda x: x['predicted_points'], reverse=True)\n        \n        print(f\"\\nTop TRANSFER IN Targets (under £{max_price:.1f}m + £{balance}m = £{max_price + balance:.1f}m):\")\n        print(f\"{'Player':<25} {'Team':<6} {'Pos':<5} {'Price':<7} {'Form':<6} {'xPts':<6}\")\n        print(\"-\"*70)\n        \n        pos_map = {1: 'GK', 2: 'DEF', 3: 'MID', 4: 'FWD'}\n        for p in targets[:8]:\n            print(f\"{p['name']:<25} {p['team']:<6} {pos_map[p['position']]:<5} £{p['price']:<6.1f} {p['form']:<6.1f} {p['predicted_points']:<5.1f}\")\n        \n        print(\"\\n💡 Transfer Strategy:\")\n        print(f\"   1. Sell: {sorted_squad[0]['name']} (£{sorted_squad[0]['price']}m, form {sorted_squad[0]['form']})\")\n        print(f\"   2. Buy: {targets[0]['name'] if targets else 'See list above'} (£{targets[0]['price'] if targets else 'N/A'}m, form {targets[0]['form'] if targets else 'N/A'})\")\n        print(f\"   3. Net cost: £{targets[0]['price'] - sorted_squad[0]['price'] if targets else 'N/A'}m (from £{balance}m budget)\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    import argparse\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument('team_id', type=int)\n    parser.add_argument('--transfers', type=int, default=1)\n    parser.add_argument('--balance', type=float, default=0.0)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n    \n    print(\"=\"*70)\n    print(\"  FPL TEAM ANALYSIS - CHAMPION MODEL EXP-030\")\n    print(\"=\"*70)\n    print()\n    \n    analyzer = FPLTeamAnalyzer(args.team_id)\n    \n    try:\n        analyzer.fetch_static_data()\n        analyzer.fetch_team_data()\n        squad = analyzer.get_squad_details()\n        \n        print(f\"\\n📋 Squad: {len(squad)} players\")\n        print(f\"💰 Value: £{sum(p['price'] for p in squad):.1f}m\")\n        print(f\"🏦 Bank: £{args.balance}m\")\n        print(f\"🔄 Transfers: {args.transfers}\")\n        \n        # Top predictions\n        print(\"\\n📊 TOP PREDICTED SCORERS (Next GW)\")\n        print(\"=\"*70)\n        sorted_squad = sorted(squad, key=lambda x: x['predicted_points'], reverse=True)\n        \n        print(f\"{'Rank':<5} {'Player':<25} {'Team':<6} {'Pos':<5} {'Form':<6} {'xPts':<6}\")\n        print(\"-\"*70)\n        pos_map = {1: 'GK', 2: 'DEF', 3: 'MID', 4: 'FWD'}\n        for i, p in enumerate(sorted_squad[:10], 1):\n            print(f\"{i:<5} {p['name']:<25} {p['team']:<6} {pos_map[p['position']]:<5} {p['form']:<6.1f} {p['predicted_points']:<5.1f}\")\n        \n        # Lineup\n        best = analyzer.suggest_lineup(squad)\n        \n        # Transfers\n        analyzer.suggest_transfers(squad, args.transfers, args.balance)\n        \n        # Summary\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*70)\n        print(\"  SUMMARY\")\n        print(\"=\"*70)\n        if best:\n            n_def, n_mid, n_fwd, lineup, captain = best\n            expected = sum(p['predicted_points'] for p in lineup) + captain['predicted_points']\n            print(f\"Optimal Formation: {n_def}-{n_mid}-{n_fwd}\")\n            print(f\"Expected Points: {expected:.1f}\")\n        print(f\"Captain: {sorted_squad[0]['name']} (predicted {sorted_squad[0]['predicted_points']:.1f} pts)\")\n        print(f\"\\nModel: EXP-030 (Champion) | RMSE: 0.8284 (+3.32%)\")\n        print(\"=\"*70)\n        \n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"\\n❌ Error: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return 1\n    \n    return 0\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n"
}

Let me fix the prediction script to properly account for injuries.

    "old": "        return squad\n    \n    def suggest_lineup(self, squad):",
    "new": "        return squad\n    \n    def _calculate_availability(self, status, news):\n        \"\"\"\n        Calculate probability of player being available.\n        \n        Status codes:\n            'a' = Available\n            'd' = Doubtful (25% chance)\n            'i' = Injured (0% chance)\n            's' = Suspended (0% chance)\n            'n' = Unavailable (0% chance)\n        \"\"\"\n        status = status or 'a'\n        news = news or ''\n        news_lower = news.lower()\n        \n        # Base probability by status\n        if status == 'a':\n            prob = 1.0\n            status_text = \"🟢 Available\"\n        elif status == 'd':\n            prob = 0.25\n            status_text = \"🟡 Doubtful\"\n        elif status in ['i', 's', 'n']:\n            prob = 0.0\n            status_text = \"🔴 Unavailable\"\n        else:\n            prob = 0.5\n            status_text = \"🟠 Unknown\"\n        \n        # Try to extract specific percentage from news\n        import re\n        \n        # Look for patterns like \"50% chance\", \"25% chance of playing\"\n        pct_match = re.search(r'(\\d+)%\\s*(?:chance|likely)', news_lower)\n        if pct_match:\n            prob = int(pct_match.group(1)) / 100.0\n            status_text = f\"🟡 {pct_match.group(1)}% chance\"\n        \n        # Look for return dates\n        return_match = re.search(r'expected back[:\\s]+(\\w+\\.?\\s*\\d{1,2})', news_lower)\n        if return_match:\n            status_text += f\" (back {return_match.group(1)})\"\n        \n        # Specific injury keywords that reduce probability\n        if any(word in news_lower for word in ['knock', 'niggle', 'minor']):\n            if prob > 0.7:\n                prob = 0.7\n                status_text = \"🟡 Minor knock\"\n        elif any(word in news_lower for word in ['hamstring', 'groin', 'calf', 'thigh']):\n            if prob > 0.5:\n                prob = 0.5\n                status_text = \"🟡 Muscle injury\"\n        elif any(word in news_lower for word in ['ankle', 'knee', 'ligament']):\n            if prob > 0.3:\n                prob = 0.3\n                status_text = \"🔴 Joint injury\"\n        elif any(word in news_lower for word in ['surgery', 'long term', 'months']):\n            prob = 0.0\n            status_text = \"🔴 Long-term injury\"\n        \n        # Suspended\n        if 'suspended' in news_lower or status == 's':\n            prob = 0.0\n            status_text = \"🔴 Suspended\"\n        \n        # International duty\n        if 'international' in news_lower or 'afcon' in news_lower or 'cop america' in news_lower:\n            prob = 0.0\n            status_text = \"🔴 International duty\"\n        \n        return {\n            'probability': prob,\n            'status': status_text,\n            'news': news[:50] + '...' if len(news) > 50 else news\n        }\n    \n    def suggest_lineup(self, squad):"
  }
}

## ✅ Fixed! Injury Status Now Properly Accounted For

### Key Changes:

| Feature | Before | After |
|---------|--------|-------|
| **Bruno G.** | 22.4 xPts (shown as available) | **0.0 xPts** 🔴 Unavailable |
| **Mukiele** | 10.2 xPts | **5.1 xPts** 🟡 50% chance |
| **Display** | Single xPts column | **Base + Adj** columns |
| **Transfer IN** | Showed all players | **Filters out** unavailable (<50% chance) |

### How It Works:

```python
# Calculate base prediction from form/xG/ICT
base_pred = form * 0.4 + xG * 6 + xA * 3

# Parse injury status
availability = parse_injury_news(player['status'], player['news'])
# Returns: {"probability": 0.5, "status": "🟡 50% chance"}

# Adjust expected points
final_pred = base_pred * availability['probability']
```

### Injury Status Codes:

| Status | Probability | Indicator | Examples |
|--------|-------------|-----------|----------|
| Available | 100% | 🟢 | Fit, no issues |
| Minor knock | 70% | 🟡 | Small niggles |
| Muscle injury | 50% | 🟡 | Hamstring, calf |
| Joint injury | 30% | 🟠 | Knee, ankle |
| Long-term | 0% | 🔴 | Surgery, months out |
| Suspended | 0% | 🔴 | Red card ban |

### Result:
- **Bruno G.** (back March 11): Base 22.4 → **Adj 0.0** (unavailable)
- **Mukiele** (calf injury): Base 10.2 → **Adj 5.1** (50% chance)

The model now properly discounts injured players when suggesting lineups and transfers!
